{"step_index":1010,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-05-16T15:57:12Z","content":"Those warnings are simply **Python Deprecation Warnings**. \n\nIn our database models (`app/models/signals.py`, `incidents.py`, etc.), we are using `datetime.utcnow` to automatically set the `created_at` timestamp. \n\nStarting in Python 3.12, `datetime.utcnow()` was officially deprecated in favor of using timezone-aware datetimes like `datetime.now(datetime.UTC)`. Because Pydantic V2 is very strict, it complains every time it generates a timestamp during our tests.\n\n**Are they important?**\nNo, not at all for this Hackathon. They won't break the application, cause any bugs, or affect the AI logic. It's just a minor library complaint. \n\nAs you requested, **we can completely ignore them and leave them as-is**.
